# Project State

## Current Status

- **Milestone v4.24 Same-Origin Storage on R2** — ROADMAP CREATED 2026-08-06. Serve every user-uploaded and platform asset from the app's own origin, backed by Cloudflare R2, so images land on the CDN that already fronts `xtimator.com` and Supabase Storage egress goes to zero. **6 phases (187-192)**, **20/20 requirements mapped** (PROXY-01..05, PROV-01..03, UPLOAD-01..04, URL-01..04, MIG-01..04), **0 orphans, 0 duplicates**. Numbering continues the global counter — v4.23 ended at Phase 186, so v4.24 starts at **Phase 187**; the parking-lot dirs 999.1/1000/1001 are out-of-band and do not affect sequencing.
- **Phase map (goal-backward, dependency-ordered):**
  - **187 R2 Provisioning & Same-Origin Asset Proxy** (PROXY-01..04, MIG-03) — five R2 buckets (`audio`/`photos`/`pdfs`/`logos`/`platform-brand`) with public access disabled and a scoped credential, plus a same-origin route streaming any object from R2 with content type preserved, a **transparent Supabase read-through fallback** so nothing 404s in either direction, traversal/unknown-bucket rejection with zero credential leakage, and immutable edge caching for public assets only. The keystone — the fallback is what makes every later phase reversible.
  - **188 Server-Wide Provider Selection Integrity** (PROV-01..03) — COMPLETE 2026-08-06 (Plans 01-05 all shipped). One provider resolution (`serverStorageBackend()` in `lib/storage/server.ts`) for every server-side read and write so `STORAGE_PROVIDER` can no longer half-apply; a 26-row exact-manifest AST census (`tests/unit/storage/storage-seam-census.test.ts`) that fails CI if a server module reintroduces a hardcoded Supabase path or a client component imports the server seam — proved capable of failing on all 4 required negative cases; WhatsApp inbound media proven write-and-read on one backend. The census itself found and closed 2 real gaps Plans 01-03 had missed (`analyze-photos.ts`, `transcribe-audio.ts` calling raw `supabase.storage.from(...)` directly).
  - **189 Browser Uploads Without Browser Credentials** (UPLOAD-01..04) — the five client upload call sites (`capture-recorder`, `inline-audio-recorder`, `photo-card`, `photo-lightbox`, `estimate-document`) move to server-issued presigned PUTs; tenant-namespaced key authorization; correct content type verified through the proxy including extension-less keys; retry + offline/queue behavior preserved. **File-disjoint — parallelizable with 188/190/191.**
  - **190 Portable Same-Origin Asset URLs** (URL-01, URL-03, URL-04) — new writes persist same-origin relative URLs (no backend hostname in any row); every surface resolves them (app UI, share pages, PDFs, email/WhatsApp), including the server-side PDF renderer with no browser origin; CSP updated and narrowed. Existing absolute rows still render unchanged — no rewrite yet.
  - **191 Object Migration & Verification** (MIG-01, MIG-02, MIG-04) — a re-runnable, twice-safe copy command with per-object count/size/content-type verification that fails loudly on mismatch, plus a cutover+rollback runbook with verified R2 settings and no real secrets.
  - **192 URL Rewrite Cutover & CDN Verification** (URL-02, PROXY-05) — the milestone's only irreversible-ish data step, sequenced last: rewrite `companies.logo_url` / `profiles.avatar_url` / price-book image URLs / platform branding+SEO rows off `*.supabase.co` with a reversible record, then prove a cold landing-page load fetches every image from `xtimator.com` with a Cloudflare cache HIT, and that removing the R2 env vars still renders everything via the Supabase fallback.
- **Dependency spine:** 187 → 188 → 190 → 191 → 192, with 189 parallelizable off 187. PROXY-02's fallback MUST precede any migration or URL rewrite. PROV MUST land before/with the migration — flipping the flag with ~20 hardcoded call sites causes split-brain writes/reads and silent 404s on WhatsApp inbound media (verified failure mode, 2026-08-05 field assessment). MIG-03 is an operator prerequisite for anything writing to R2, hence front-loaded into 187.
- **Do not re-plan what is proven:** `lib/storage/s3-provider.ts` works against R2 **unmodified** — `scripts/storage-smoke.ts` passed upload → signed URL → in-process download → HTTP fetch of the signed URL → delete, with `S3_REGION=auto`, `S3_FORCE_PATH_STYLE=true`, endpoint `https://<account-id>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`. No phase spends effort re-validating the provider. The Cloudflare CDN layer is already live (`docs/CLOUDFLARE-CDN.md`) — this milestone only makes images reach it.
- **Scale flag:** production storage today is **51 objects / 14.3 MB** (photos 11 MB, platform-brand 2.8 MB, logos 55 kB, audio 55 kB, pdfs 0). Transfer volume and time are non-issues — **Phase 191 is dominated by correctness and per-object verification**, not throughput, batching, windowing, or resumability. The trigger is **egress, not stored volume**: ~1.9 MB of landing-page images per cold visit, ~1.9 GB per 1 000 cold visits.
- **Reversibility is a hard requirement:** removing the R2 env vars must return the app to Supabase with no code change and no data migration — Phase 192 proves it rather than assuming it. S3 credentials must never reach the browser. No real secrets in the repo (placeholders only; real values in `.env.local` and Coolify). Storage keys keep `{companyId}/{type}/{timestamp}-{filename}` — re-keying is out of scope.
- **Phase 191 COMPLETE (2026-08-07)** — MIG-01/MIG-02/MIG-04 all shipped. `npm run migrate:r2` / `npm run migrate:r2 -- --verify-only` executed against production: 55 objects copied, 0 failed; idempotency proven (second run copied=0); a deliberate corruption drill caught and restored. `docs/STORAGE-MIGRATION.md` carries the authoritative cutover/rollback runbook, gated by `tests/unit/storage/storage-migration-runbook.test.ts`. Phase 190 (Plans 02/03) still in progress. Next: finish Phase 190, then `/gsd:plan-phase 192`.

Closed the 10 severity-ranked findings: signed-estimate mutability with zero snapshot/audit (CRITICAL — snapshot-on-sign + freeze-on-send + estimate_updated events, Phase 164); non-atomic saveEstimate session-poisoning (single transactional RPC + version authority + preview parity, Phase 165); missing AI fetch timeouts + 4096-token truncation + 4 pricing fields missing from the live OpenRouter tool schema + coarse quality gate (Phase 166); unmetered refine + client-declared-duration exploit + dead vision cost roll-up + Whisper retry re-pay (Phase 167); photos 21-50 silently dropped + captions never reaching generation + whole-batch failure on one bad photo (Phase 168); single-shot uploads with memory-only blobs + false offline banner + permanent storage orphans (Phase 169); refine stale-read race + no review-before-apply + full-tree id churn (Phase 170, closed by 170-01's shared `mergeRefinement` two-pass identity-preserving merge + flush-before-refine + review-before-apply). Sequencing executed as planned: 164 → 165 strictly sequential (both rewrote `lib/actions/estimate.ts`); 166/167/168/169 file-disjoint and parallelized across concurrent sessions; 170 depended on 165's id_map/editEpoch and landed last. Locked decisions in REQUIREMENTS.md: freeze policy (sent_at OR signature OR client_response → typed `estimate_locked`; presentation settings stay editable), money stays float-major-units this milestone (parity fixes the dollar-scale bug; minor-units deferred FUT-01), structured photo extraction deferred (FUT-02), TUS deferred (FUT-04), hand-rolled fail-soft IndexedDB, refine review-before-apply v1 = summary diff not per-line accept/reject (FUT-03 deferred). Regression contracts held throughout (verified per-phase, never weakened): Inngest idempotency/concurrency, GUARD-03 server math, price-research evidence gate, prompt-injection hardening, cross-tenant scoping. Pure hardening — no new AI features shipped. Numbering continued the global counter — v4.18 ended at Phase 163, v4.19 ran Phases 164-170. 13 plans total, all COMPLETE. See ROADMAP.md's Phase 164-170 sections for full per-plan detail and every `*-SUMMARY.md` for exhaustive per-plan record.

- **Milestone**: v4.13 Annual Billing — ROADMAP CREATED 2026-06-25. **5 phases (141-145)**, **5/5 requirements mapped** (ANN-01..ANN-05), **no orphans**. Add a discounted ANNUAL subscription option while keeping AI credit distribution MONTHLY for every interval — annual changes price + billing cadence only, never the rate at which credits flow. The load-bearing change is decoupling the monthly credit grant from the invoice cadence: a monthly Inngest cron + a `grant:{companyId}:{YYYY-MM}` company-month idempotency key SHARED with the `invoice.paid` webhook → exactly one grant per company per calendar month for any interval. Source: SEED-038. Numbering continues the global counter — v4.12 ended at Phase 140, so v4.13 starts at **Phase 141**.
- **Phase plan (goal-backward, 5 phases — configurable price foundation, then the load-bearing grant decouple, then checkout + seat interval, then the UI toggle):**
  - **141 Configurable Annual Pricing** (ANN-01) — extend `BillingConfig`/`DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG` with `tiers[tier].subscriptionPriceAnnualCents` (per-tier) + `seatPriceAnnualCents` (global) as null-safe, deep-merge-tolerant calibration placeholders; mirror in the admin zod schema; surface both as editable super-admin billing-panel fields. The foundation the annual price plugs into; nothing hardcoded; independent of the cron.
  - **142 Monthly Credit Grant Decouple** (ANN-02) — THE load-bearing phase. Change the `invoice.paid` grant idempotency key from `event.id` to `grant:{companyId}:{YYYY-MM}`; add an Inngest monthly cron (`lib/inngest/functions/monthly-credit-grant.ts`, mirroring `cleanup-audio`) granting `monthlyCreditGrant` to active paying companies once per company-month using the SAME key, reusing the idempotent never-throw `grantCredits`. Exactly one grant per company per calendar month for ALL intervals; monthly sub → webhook grants (cron no-ops), annual sub → webhook grants month 1, cron grants months 2-12. A retrocompat regression test (monthly subs, NO double-grant across webhook + cron) is the gate.
  - **143 Annual Checkout** (ANN-03) — `create-checkout-session` accepts `billingInterval: 'month' | 'year'` (default `'month'`), selects the matching annual Stripe Price ID (new env `STRIPE_PRICE_PRO_ANNUAL` / `STRIPE_PRICE_BUSINESS_ANNUAL`, PLACEHOLDERS ONLY), stores `billing_interval` in session/subscription metadata. The no-interval / `'month'` path stays byte-identical. Depends on 141 (the annual price/Stripe linkage).
  - **144 Interval-Aware Seat Billing** (ANN-04) — make `syncSubscriptionSeatItem` read the subscription's interval and match it (replacing the hardcoded `recurring: { interval: 'month' }`), using `seatPriceAnnualCents` (inline `price_data`) for annual. Monthly orgs unchanged; gated by `enforcementEnabled`. Depends on 141 (`seatPriceAnnualCents`); builds on the v4.12 seat billing; independent of checkout (reads the live subscription's interval).
  - **145 Pricing UI Toggle** (ANN-05) — the pricing cards (`tier-cards-grid.tsx` + `tier-card.tsx`) gain a Monthly/Annual toggle showing the annual price, the DERIVED "save X%" badge, and the per-month equivalent; the selected interval threads into the upgrade/checkout action. Mobile-safe; i18n en/pt/es via runtime `t()`. Depends on 141 (the annual price to display) + 143 (the checkout interval to thread into).
- **Dependency spine:** 141 (configurable annual price + seat price — the foundation) + 142 (the grant decouple — independent, parallelizable with 141) → 143 (annual checkout, depends on 141) + 144 (interval-aware seat billing, depends on 141, independent of 143) → 145 (pricing UI toggle, depends on 141 + 143). 142 is the heart of the milestone — get the company-month dedup right and the rest is mechanical.
- **Locked guardrails (SEED-038 + REQUIREMENTS.md + PROJECT.md):** Credits stay MONTHLY for EVERY interval — annual is only a price discount (same tier, same `monthlyCreditGrant`, same seats). The company-month key `grant:{companyId}:{YYYY-MM}` is the SINGLE dedup authority shared by the webhook AND the cron → exactly one grant per company per calendar month, NO double-grant. ZERO hardcoded billing numbers — `subscriptionPriceAnnualCents` (per-tier) + `seatPriceAnnualCents` (global) live in `billing_config`, read via `getBillingConfig()`, editable without a deploy; the displayed discount % is DERIVED (`1 − annual/(12×monthly)`), never stored; no annual price / discount % / Stripe Price ID may be a constant in application code. The base annual charge uses pre-created Stripe Price IDs (env `STRIPE_PRICE_PRO_ANNUAL` / `STRIPE_PRICE_BUSINESS_ANNUAL` — PLACEHOLDERS ONLY in every doc, never a real ID or key); seat annual uses inline `price_data` straight from `seatPriceAnnualCents`. Interval is selected at checkout (`billingInterval`, default `'month'`) and threaded through metadata; the seat sync matches the subscription interval. Charging stays gated by `enforcementEnabled` / live-mode discipline (display can ship anytime). Retrocompat is load-bearing: default interval `'month'`, the existing monthly path byte-identical, a regression test locks the no-double-grant invariant. Status (128-01, Wave 1 — MGEN-01/MPAR-01): shipped — **Phase 128 COMPLETE (1/1 plan) — the FINAL plan of v4.10; the thin, non-destructive reconciliation that routes the EXISTING MCP `create_estimate` through the v4.9 channel-neutral `createEstimate` so WhatsApp = web chat = MCP all dispatch generation through ONE core (MGEN-01), proven by a static binding test (MPAR-01).** **Task 1 (Option A — the single justified widening of the neutral fn):** `lib/agent-tools/create-estimate.ts` now mints a CHANNEL-NAMESPACED Inngest idempotency id — `estimate-${args.channel ? `${args.channel}-` : ''}${args.projectId}-${requestId}` → `estimate-<channel>-<projectId>-<requestId>` when a channel is passed, `estimate-<projectId>-<requestId>` otherwise. `channel` is already a NEUTRAL param so folding it into the id adds NO forbidden token — the neutrality gate (`tests/unit/agent-tools/neutrality.test.ts`) stays green. The neutral test asserts all three id forms (mcp→`/^estimate-mcp-proj-1-/`, web→`/^estimate-web-proj-1-/`, none→`/^estimate-proj-1-/` not `(web|mcp)`). **Task 2 (MGEN-01):** `lib/mcp/tools/write.ts` `handleCreateEstimate` REPLACED its inline `inngest.send(EVENT_ESTIMATE_GENERATE)` block with `const { jobId } = await createEstimate({ companyId: auth.company_id, projectId: input.project_id, prompts:[input.prompt], ...(language?), channel:'mcp' })` — mirroring `lib/chat/tools.ts`'s `channel:'web'` binding. KEPT verbatim: the `mcp:write` scope gate, the project-ownership lookup (service client → `.from('projects').eq('id').maybeSingle()` → notFound on missing/cross-company), and the `{ job_id: jobId, status:'queued', message:'…check_job_status…' }` envelope. DROPPED the now-dead imports (`randomUUID`, `inngest`, `EVENT_ESTIMATE_GENERATE`, `EstimateGeneratePayload`); `check_job_status` is untouched (it uses `fetch` + `INNGEST_SIGNING_KEY`, not those). The channel:'mcp' delegation + Task-1's namespaced id keep the behavior guard `tests/unit/mcp-create-estimate.test.ts` GREEN BYTE-UNCHANGED (the real neutral fn runs against the same leaf-mocked `@/lib/inngest/client` — NO `vi.mock('@/lib/agent-tools')`, Pitfall 5). **Task 3 (MPAR-01):** NEW `tests/unit/mcp/mcp-generation-parity.test.ts` (static `readFileSync`+grep, modeled on neutrality.test.ts — no DB/mocks/secrets): (1) write.ts imports `from '@/lib/agent-tools'` + contains `createEstimate(`; (2) write.ts does NOT contain `EVENT_ESTIMATE_GENERATE`; (3) three-channel convergence — chat/`createEstimate(`, mcp/`createEstimate(`, whatsapp `lib/whatsapp/confirm-actions.ts`/`generateEstimateForProject(`. 3/3 green. **2 DEVIATIONS (both blocking/robustness, in the task commits):** (1) Rule 3 — write.ts had TWO doc-comment mentions of `EVENT_ESTIMATE_GENERATE` after the code was deleted; reworded both so `grep -c EVENT_ESTIMATE_GENERATE` → 0 and the MPAR-01 negative assertion passes (1241e84a); (2) Rule 2 — added an explicit `toContain("from '@/lib/agent-tools'")` alongside the regex in the parity test so the acceptance grep is grep-discoverable (88bbd166). No source behavior changed. **VERIFICATION:** MGEN-01 suites (mcp-create-estimate + check-job-status + tool-registry + agent-tools/create-estimate) all green; MPAR-01 + neutrality green; FULL `npx vitest run` → **336 files passed | 2354 passed**, the ONLY fail is the KNOWN parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` GET-405 flake (re-confirmed 8/8 GREEN in isolation, touches no Phase-128 file). `tsc --noEmit` clean on both Phase-128 source files (no dead imports). No migration, no new dep, no secret. 3 atomic commits (be2b6d1f feat namespaced-id, 1241e84a feat MCP delegation, 88bbd166 test parity); all normal hooked IN-PLACE (gitleaks ran each, no `--no-verify`), no leaks. MGEN-01 + MPAR-01 marked complete. **Phase 128 now 1/1 — COMPLETE; v4.10 (Phases 127-128, 6/6 requirements) — ALL phases shipped, Multi-Channel Core track CLOSED.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 128`, then `/gsd:complete-milestone` for v4.10. Status (126-02, Wave 2 — CHATMETER-02): shipped — **Phase 126 COMPLETE (2/2 plans) — the LAST plan of Phase 126 and milestone v4.9; the page-level entitlement UX + the owner-only / never-customer-facing structural fence.** **`components/chat/chat-upgrade-prompt.tsx`:** a self-contained `'use client'` component mirroring the ChatWorkspace shell (same `h-[calc(100vh-4rem)]` + MessageSquare header) so it fits the chat surface — a centered card (Sparkles glyph), copy via `useTranslation()`, primary `<Button asChild><Link href="/settings/billing"></Button>` (the literal `/settings/billing` matches the route 403 `upgradeUrl`). No props. **`app/(app)/chat/[[...id]]/page.tsx` gate:** after `userId` and BEFORE any conversation query — `getActiveCompanyId()` → a DIRECT `requireServiceClient().from('companies').select('tier').eq('id', companyId!).maybeSingle()` read → `getEntitlements(tier).chatEnabled`; false → `return <ChatUpgradePrompt />`; existing ChatWorkspace path byte-unchanged below. **PITFALL avoided (load-bearing):** the active-company helper does NOT select `tier` — reading it there → undefined → `getEntitlements('free')` → paying users wrongly blocked; the page does its OWN tier read (the app/(app)/layout.tsx pattern). The page-level prompt is the PRIMARY 403 affordance — the global UpgradeModal only intercepts 402, NOT the route's 403. **`tests/unit/chat/chat-page-gate.test.tsx`:** renders the awaited async RSC with mocked auth/active-company/chainable-service-client (per-test `tierRow`)/queries/history-mapper + `ChatWorkspace`→`WORKSPACE_SENTINEL`, using the REAL `@/lib/entitlements`; 3 cases — free→prompt (`/settings/billing` + `Upgrade your plan`, no sentinel), pro→workspace (sentinel, no billing), null-row→fallback-free→prompt. **`tests/unit/chat/chat-access-scope.test.ts`:** pure `readFileSync`/`readdirSync` (no DB/secrets, modeled on chat-ui-scope.test.ts) — (1) the chat page references `ChatWorkspace`; (2) NONE of the 11 public/non-(app) route groups ((auth),(capture),admin,blog,demo,estimate,oauth,offline,onboarding,privacy-policy,terms-of-service) reference `ChatWorkspace`/`@/components/chat`/`/api/chat`; (3) `app/api/chat/route.ts` contains BOTH `getClaims` (owner auth) AND `chat_not_on_plan` (tier gate). **0 deviations** — plan executed exactly as written; no stubs (gate reads real tier; prompt link wired). grep: `getEntitlements(tier).chatEnabled`=1, `select('tier')`=1, `getActiveCompany(`=0 (uses `getActiveCompanyId` + direct read), `ChatUpgradePrompt`=2. `npx vitest run tests/unit/chat/chat-page-gate.test.tsx tests/unit/chat/chat-access-scope.test.ts` → 2 files / 6 passed; `npx vitest run tests/unit/chat tests/unit/entitlements.test.ts` → 17 files / 118 passed (Phase-125 chat-ui-scope.test.ts still green). **FULL `npx vitest run` → 335 files passed | 3 skipped, 2335 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** — the known parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` flake did NOT even surface. `tsc --noEmit` no new errors in the two touched source files. No migration, no new dep, no secret. 2 atomic commits (c6aa5a45 feat prompt+gate, bf9acfe6 test page-gate+scope-fence); all normal hooked IN-PLACE (gitleaks ran each, no `--no-verify`), no leaks. CHATMETER-02 marked complete. **Phase 126 now 2/2 — COMPLETE; v4.9 all 5 phases (122-126) shipped.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 126`, then `/gsd:complete-milestone` for v4.9. See 126-02-SUMMARY.md. 'new'}` resets `useChat` across switches. **`components/chat/chat-sidebar.tsx` (CHATUI-02):** New chat button (Plus) + newest-first conversation rows as `next/link href='/chat/<id>'` (RSC reload seeds history), `title ?? t('Untitled chat')` + relative time, active `bg-muted/60`, empty state. **`components/chat/chat-message.tsx` (CHATUI-01):** `message.parts.map` switch — `type==='text'` → markdown bubble (assistant, react-markdown+remark-gfm) / plain bubble (user, by role); `tool-*`/`dynamic-tool` → `ChatToolPart`; else null. Status (123-02, Wave 2 — CHATDB-02): shipped — **Phase 123 COMPLETE (2/2 plans) — the LAST plan; `lib/queries/chat.ts`, the four tenant + owner-scoped chat query helpers that make the Phase-123 tables ready-to-consume.** A verbatim-pattern MIRROR of `lib/queries/whatsapp-inbox.ts`: service client scoped to `getActiveCompanyId()` resolved INTERNALLY (no trusted tenant arg), every query re-scoped by `company_id` (+ `user_id` for owner-only conversations), whatsapp-inbox guard style (early `[]`/`null` returns on no-company / no-service-client). **4 helpers:** `listConversations(userId)` → `.eq('company_id', companyId).eq('user_id', userId).order('updated_at',{ascending:false}).limit(200)` (newest-updated first; `[]` without touching the service client when no company); `getConversationWithMessages(conversationId, userId)` → conversation `.eq('id').eq('company_id').eq('user_id').maybeSingle()` then `null` (and NO chat_messages query) when not found/owned, else messages `.eq('conversation_id').order('created_at',{ascending:true}).limit(500)`; `createConversation(userId, title=null)` → `.insert({company_id,user_id,title}).select('*').single()`; `appendMessage({conversationId,role,parts,attachments?})` → inserts into `chat_messages` with the DENORMALIZED `company_id` + `parts ?? []`/`attachments ?? null`, THEN bumps `chat_conversations.updated_at` (scoped `.eq('id').eq('company_id')`) so new activity re-sorts to the top (mirrors whatsapp `bumpConversation` — Pitfall 5); no-op `null` when no company. Row types declared (`ChatRole`, `ChatConversationRow`, `ChatMessageRow`, `ChatThread`). **Architectural posture:** the helper path is service-role + explicit `.eq` scoping; the Phase-123-01 RLS stays defense-in-depth — ONE consistent pattern Phase 124's route handler writes through without RLS friction. The WRITE helpers shipped NOW (not deferred to Phase 124) so the tables are ready-to-consume. **TDD RED→GREEN:** NEW `tests/unit/chat/chat-queries.test.ts` (7 it()) over a chainable service-client mock recording every `.eq(col,val)` + `.from(table)` + `.insert`/`.update` payload per query, mocking BOTH `@/lib/queries/active-company` and `@/lib/supabase/service`; the literal `'company-SECRET'` is the cross-tenant tripwire asserted on the list + conversation-fetch queries; `appendMessage` asserts BOTH the message insert (with `company_id`) AND the `updated_at` bump on the parent. Task 1 RED (`Failed to resolve import "@/lib/queries/chat"` — module absent, correct Wave-0 state); Task 2 GREEN (7/7). `grep -c 'getActiveCompanyId()' lib/queries/chat.ts` → 5 (4 helpers + 1 doc); no caller-supplied company id trusted. **0 deviations** — content prescribed verbatim by the plan; no REFACTOR needed; no stubs. Scope fence honored: no route handler / AI SDK / UI (Phases 124/125 OUT). FULL `npx vitest run` → **321 files passed | 3 skipped, 2251 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (baseline 123-01 320 files / 2244; +1 file / +7 — exactly this new suite, no regressions; the known parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` flake did not surface). `tsc --noEmit` clean on `lib/queries/chat.ts`. No migration, no new dep, no secret. 2 atomic commits (8c620d04 RED test, 6ceaa50 GREEN helpers); all normal hooked IN-PLACE (gitleaks ran each, no `--no-verify`), no leaks. Three actions: **createCompanyEntry** — auth gate → `companyKnowledgeEntrySchema.safeParse` → `embed(title+'\n\n'+body)` BEFORE the write (KOVL-02; embed throw → `ok:false`, NO insert) → AUTHED `supabase.from('knowledge_entries').insert({scope:'company', company_id:company.id, industry_id:null, title, body, source:?? null, embedding: toVectorLiteral(emb)})`; **updateCompanyEntry** — fetches existing `title,body` scoped `.eq('id').eq('company_id').maybeSingle()` (defense-in-depth on top of RLS), `needsEmbed = !existing || title/body changed` → re-embeds ONLY on change (source-only edit skips embed; embed throw on a changed body → `ok:false`, no update), `.update(payload).eq('id').eq('company_id')` with NO `industry_id` key (keeps it NULL); **deleteCompanyEntry** — `.delete().eq('id').eq('company_id')`. Local `toVectorLiteral = JSON.stringify` DUPLICATED (not extracted — three-axis divergence per 120-RESEARCH). **THE SECURITY INVARIANT (load-bearing):** uses the RLS-bound AUTHED `createClient()` — the Phase-117 `knowledge_entries_company_insert/update/delete` policies (gated by `company_members`) enforce tenant isolation; the file imports NONE of `requireServiceClient`/`requireAdmin`/`logAdminAction` (only doc-comment mentions), NO audit log (mirrors estimate-template), `scope='company'`+`company_id`+`industry_id:null`. Using the service client here would bypass RLS — explicitly avoided and tested. **TDD RED→GREEN:** NEW `tests/unit/settings/knowledge-overlay-actions.test.ts` (10 cases) retargets the 119 curation test to the authed client + company scope — mocks `@/lib/supabase/server` (authed createClient w/ getClaims+from), `@/lib/queries/active-company`, `@/lib/demo/guard`, `@/lib/knowledge/embed`, `next/cache`, and mocks `requireServiceClient` to THROW as a never-called regression guard. Status (119-02, Wave 2 — KCUR-03): shipped — the super-admin industry KB **bulk-import data path**: a CSV parser + a batch-embed/bulk-insert action that seeds an entire industry's KB in one operation. **`lib/csv/knowledge-import.ts`** — `parseKnowledgeCsv(file) -> KnowledgeParseOutcome`, a simplified near-copy of `parsePriceBookCsv`: 1 MB size cap (`too_large`), extension/MIME check (`wrong_type`), `Papa.parse` with `header:true, skipEmptyLines:'greedy', transformHeader` (trim+lowercase+BOM-strip), required `title,body` cols (`missing_columns`), >1000 rows (`too_many_rows`). Per-row pushes `missing_title`/`missing_body`; in-file title dupes (case-insensitive) FLAGGED not errored. Never enables the papaparse worker thread (Pitfall 3 — doc comments reworded so the negative grep stays clean). Exports `REQUIRED_HEADERS`/`MAX_ROWS`/`MAX_BYTES`/`KnowledgeImportRow`/`ParsedKnowledgeRow`/`KnowledgeParseOutcome`. **`bulkImportEntries(industryId, rows)` appended to `app/admin/knowledge/actions.ts`** (mirrors importPriceBookItems re-validate→single bulk .insert): `requireAdmin()` FIRST (only access control — service client bypasses RLS), `isKnownIndustry(industryId)` gate (`ok:false`, no embed/insert on unknown), server re-validate (`r.title?.trim() && r.body?.trim()` drops empties; 0 valid → `ok:false`), `embedMany(valid.map(title+'\n\n'+body))` BEFORE the write — a throw → `ok:false` and NO insert (Pitfall 4, transactional feel, no partial/NULL-embedding rows), then a SINGLE `requireServiceClient().from('knowledge_entries').insert(...)` pinning `scope:'industry'`/`industry_id`/`company_id:null`/`embedding: toVectorLiteral(embeddings[i])` (Plan-01's pgvector serialization reused). `revalidatePath` + `void logAdminAction({action:'knowledge_entry.save', targetId: industryId, metadata:{imported, industry_id}})`. `KnowledgeActionResult` success arm widened to `{ok:true; imported?:number}`. One import = one industry (chosen in UI, never in the CSV). **TDD:** `knowledge-import.test.ts` (13 — parse/classify, BOM, case+order-independent headers, source-optional-null, the 4 fatals, dup flag, rowNumber, constants) GREEN; `knowledge-bulk-import.test.ts` RED→GREEN (6 — embedMany-once + single bulk .insert with scope/industry_id/company_id/embedding asserted, re-validate drops empty-body → 2 inserted, 0-valid → ok:false no embed/insert, embedMany-throws → ok:false no insert, unknown industry → ok:false neither called, non-admin gate). Status (117-01, Wave 1 — KB-01/02/03): shipped — **the v4.8 Industry Knowledge Base FOUNDATION: one idempotent, authored-only migration + one static SQL-contract test.** NEW `supabase/migrations/20260625000001_phase117_knowledge_entries.sql` (filename sorts strictly after the newest `20260624000004_phase112_credit_ledger.sql`): (1) `create extension if not exists vector with schema extensions` (pgvector, idempotent); (2) `public.knowledge_entries` — `id uuid pk`, `scope text not null check (scope in ('industry','company'))`, nullable `industry_id text` (a `lib/industries.ts` code-side id, NOT a FK) + `company_id uuid references public.companies(id) on delete cascade`, `title`/`body` not null, nullable `source`, **`embedding vector(1536)` NULLABLE** (text-embedding-3-small; DORMANT — nothing writes it until Phase 118), `created_at`/`updated_at timestamptz not null default now()`, and a scope-keys CHECK (`industry => industry_id not null and company_id is null` OR `company => company_id not null`); (3) HNSW cosine index `knowledge_entries_embedding_hnsw_idx using hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops)` (builds on the empty curated table — no IVFFlat training step); (4) **DUAL RLS on one table via permissive-policy OR semantics** — `enable row level security` + ONE `knowledge_entries_select` policy OR-ing `scope='industry'` (readable to ALL authenticated — neutral; the `industries[]` relevance filter is a Phase-118 `retrieve()` WHERE, NOT RLS) with the company arm `scope='company' and company_id in (select company_members.company_id from company_members where company_members.user_id = (select auth.uid()))`; plus company-scoped `for insert/update/delete to authenticated` policies (KB-03) each gated by the same `company_members` subquery. **KB-02 service-role-only industry writes = the ABSENCE of any `scope='industry'` write policy** (the service role bypasses RLS; a tenant literally cannot write an industry row). Mirrors `price_research_cache` (neutral/service-role posture) + `credit_ledger`/phase82 (company_members subquery + `(select auth.uid())` idiom). **Phase-82 invariant holds: zero `companies.user_id` references.** Idempotent: `CREATE EXTENSION/TABLE/INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` + `DROP POLICY IF EXISTS` before each `CREATE POLICY`. NEW `tests/unit/knowledge/knowledge-entries-migration.test.ts` (15 assertions, new `tests/unit/knowledge/` dir) — pure `readFileSync` + regex, no DB/secrets, mirroring `credit-ledger-migration.test.ts` (with `stripComments` for the no-`companies.user_id` + no-`WITH CHECK scope='industry'` negative assertions). **TDD RED→GREEN:** Task 1 the test went RED-by-missing-migration (`ENOENT`, not a syntax/import error — correct Wave-0 state); Task 2 the migration turned it fully GREEN (15/15). Per-wave gate `npx vitest run tests/unit/knowledge tests/unit/estimate/price-research-cache-migration.test.ts tests/unit/billing/credit-ledger-migration.test.ts` → 34/34 (neighbors did not regress). The table ships DORMANT (no app code embeds/retrieves until Phase 118). Status (116-02, Wave 2 — CALIB-02): shipped — **Phase 116 COMPLETE (2/2 plans) — the LAST plan of the LAST phase of v4.7.** Plan-01's pure margin-invariant validator is now WIRED to the single enforcement write path — the CALIB-02 proof. **The charge-on gate (Task 2, GREEN):** `app/admin/integrations/actions.ts` imports `validateMarginInvariant` + `TierMarginResult` from `@/lib/billing/calibration`; inside `saveBillingConfig`, AFTER `safeParse` and BEFORE `requireServiceClient()`, a block runs `validateMarginInvariant(parsed.data)` ONLY when `parsed.data.enforcementEnabled` is true and `return { ok:false, message }` (naming the failing tier(s) + `${(ratio*100).toFixed(0)}% > 30%`) with NO upsert when `!check.pass`. The upsert/invalidate/revalidate/audit logic is byte-identical — the gate only short-circuits a failing flip. Enforcement-OFF saves are NEVER gated (illustrative numbers always saveable while charging is off). **The wiring proof (Task 1, RED→GREEN):** NEW `tests/unit/admin/charge-on-gate.test.ts` reuses the `billing-config-save.test.ts` mocks but DOES NOT mock `@/lib/billing/calibration` — the REAL pure validator runs, proving the actual gate logic not a stub. Three groups: REJECT (DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG pro 0.69/business 0.67 FAIL + enforcement:true → ok:false, `upsertMock` NOT called, `lastUpsertPayload` null — the CALIB-02 proof); ALLOW (calibrated pro 1000@$29 ratio ~0.077 / business 3000@$99 ratio ~0.067 + enforcement:true → upsert once, metadata.enforcementEnabled true); NEVER gate OFF (failing defaults + enforcement:false → upsert once). RED initially (the current action upserts the failing config), GREEN after the gate. **Ops tooling (Task 3):** NEW `scripts/analyze-ai-cost.mjs` — operator-run (NOT CI), aggregates `ai_cost_events` `AVG`/`PERCENTILE_CONT(0.5)`/`PERCENTILE_CONT(0.9)`/`COUNT` per `operation_type` with `WHERE real_cost_usd IS NOT NULL` (Phase-110 null-vs-0 — NULL never coerced to 0), `console.table` output, reads `process.env.DATABASE_URL` ONLY (NO secrets), session-pooler :5432 + ssl skeleton copied from `check-pipeline-events-table.mjs`. NEW `.planning/phases/116-calibration-charge-on-validation/CALIBRATION-RUNBOOK.md` documents collect→analyze→set→validate→flip + the margin-invariant formula + marks the current `DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG` numbers ILLUSTRATIVE / enforcement OFF + a usage-profile table slot; explicitly says DO NOT flip in this phase (no production data). **0 deviations** — plan executed exactly as written. Notably NO BILLCFG-03 dormancy-guard allowlist edit was needed: the gate imports the PURE `validateMarginInvariant` (validates a config passed IN), so `actions.ts` is not a new `getBillingConfig` consumer — the guard stayed green (17/17) without touching the allowlist. `npx vitest run tests/unit/admin/charge-on-gate.test.ts tests/unit/admin/billing-config-save.test.ts` → 8/8. FULL `npx vitest run` → **298 files passed | 3 skipped, 2110 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (baseline 115-02 296/2095; +2 files) — no regressions; the known parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` flake did not surface. `tsc --noEmit` clean on `actions.ts`. No migration, no env var, no secret. `enforcementEnabled` is NOT flipped on anywhere; `DEFAULT_BILLING_CONFIG` untouched (fails the invariant by design — correct; the gate prevents an unsafe flip until calibrated numbers are set). The milestone completes SAFELY with enforcement OFF; the gate makes a future unsafe flip impossible; the runbook documents the real flip for later. 3 atomic commits (9223ecd6 RED test, bae5cd7 GREEN gate, bb99c28 script+runbook); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks. CALIB-02 marked complete. **Phase 116 now 2/2 plans — COMPLETE. This is the FINAL phase of v4.7.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 116`, then `/gsd:complete-milestone` for v4.7. See 116-02-SUMMARY.md.

Status (115-02, Wave 2 — CREDITUI-01/02): shipped — **Phase 115 COMPLETE (2/2 plans) — the LAST plan; the owner-facing credit balance UX is fully wired.** The owner-facing UI consuming Plan-01's `getCreditOverview`. **Three new billing components (server-renderable except the client button):** NEW `components/billing/credit-balance-card.tsx` `CreditBalanceCard({ balance, lowBalanceThresholds })` — glass `<Card variant="glass">` mirroring the Usage card: `<CreditCard>` header + `<T>Credits</T>`, the balance headline (`balance.toLocaleString()` `font-mono text-2xl` + `<T>credits</T>`), a STATIC guidance line (`<T>Roughly speaking, an estimate uses about 10–15 credits.</T>` — SEED-035 phrasing, NOT computed from config = fake precision before Phase-116 calibration), and a low/zero warning gated on `const isLow = lowBalanceThresholds.length > 0 && balance <= Math.max(...lowBalanceThresholds)` (empty-array guard → false): an amber `data-testid="credit-low-warning"` region (mirrors trial-banner `AlertTriangle`+amber) with INFORMATIONAL copy + `<TopUpButton packIndex={0}>` inside a `<Link href="/settings/billing?topup=1">` + an `<T>Upgrade plan</T>` `<Link href="/settings/billing">`. NEW `components/billing/credit-history-list.tsx` `CreditHistoryList({ rows })` (type from `@/lib/queries/credits`) — glass `<T>Recent activity</T>` card, each row → human label (`reason` grant/topup/adjust first, else `operation_type` estimate/photo_batch/audio_minutes/price_research, fallback `Usage`) + SIGNED delta (`+`/`` prefix, green-positive/muted-negative, `font-mono tabular-nums`) + `toLocaleDateString('en-US',{dateStyle:'medium'})`; empty → `<T>No credit activity yet.</T>`. `real_cost_usd`/`markup` are not even on the row type (Plan-01 owner-safe projection — defense in depth). NEW `components/billing/top-up-button.tsx` (`'use client'`) `TopUpButton({ packIndex })` — mirrors `upgrade-buttons.tsx`: POSTs `/api/billing/create-topup-session` `{ packIndex }` → `window.location.href = data.url`, toast on fail, disabled while pending; client sends ONLY packIndex (credits/price resolved server-side, Phase-113 Pitfall 4). **Topbar chip:** NEW `components/app-shell/credit-chip.tsx` (`'use client'`) `CreditChip({ balance })` — compact `<Link href="/settings/billing">` `<Coins>` + `balance.toLocaleString()` + `credits` (lg+), h-9 muted styling. `components/app-shell/topbar.tsx` gains `creditBalance?: number` on `TopbarProps`, rendering `{typeof creditBalance === 'number' && <CreditChip balance={creditBalance} />}` before `<NotificationBell />`. **Mount + thread (no second query):** `app/(app)/settings/billing/page.tsx` after `getBillingData` does `const company = await getActiveCompany()` (guard `if (!company) redirect('/onboarding')`) → `const credits = await getCreditOverview(company.id)`, and inserts a two-col grid `<CreditBalanceCard/> <CreditHistoryList/>` BELOW the count-based usage grid + ABOVE `TierCardsGrid` — the "Usage This Month" card byte-unchanged (**MIG-01 additive parallel run**). `app/(app)/layout.tsx` widened the EXISTING `billingRow` companies select `'tier, tier_trial_ends_at'` → `'tier, tier_trial_ends_at, credit_balance'` (NO new query, Pitfall 4) and threads `creditBalance={billingRow.data?.credit_balance ?? 0}` into `<Topbar/>`. **Wave-0 TDD:** NEW `tests/unit/billing/credit-balance-card.test.tsx` (5 — balance shown; static 10/15 guidance with no `$`/markup/token; low [40 ≤ max 200] warning + `/settings/billing?topup=1` + `/settings/billing` CTA with no blocked/denied/cannot; healthy [5000] hides warning; zero shows warning + top-up CTA) — `<T>`+`TopUpButton` mocked to passthroughs so copy assertions run on plain rendered HTML. **CARDINAL RULES:** no cost math in the rendered owner UX (grep `$`/`real_cost_usd`/`markup`/`token` against rendered output clean — source matches are doc-comments + a className `${` only; Test 2 asserts `innerHTML` has no `$`/markup/token); INFORMATIONAL copy only (no blocked/denied/cannot in rendered warning — Test 3); i18n via `<T>`/`t()` throughout; one `from('companies')` in the layout (chip needs no extra query). **0 deviations** — plan executed exactly as written. Pre-existing tsc errors in 4 unrelated test files (es2018 regex-flag + mock typing — `refine-shared-prompt`, `observability`, `step-runner`, `generate-estimate-job`) are out-of-scope (not caused by this plan, no touched file affected). `npx vitest run tests/unit/billing/credit-balance-card.test.tsx` → 5/5. FULL `npx vitest run` → **296 files passed | 3 skipped, 2095 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (baseline 115-01 295/2090; +1 file / +5) — no regressions; known parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` flake did not surface. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` → no errors in any of the 8 touched/created source files. No migration, no env var, no secret (`credit_balance` is a pre-existing Phase-112 companies column). **Task 4 (checkpoint:human-verify) AUTO-APPROVED** (auto-mode `workflow.auto_advance=true` + project memory standing instruction) — proceeded without pausing; live `/settings/billing` visual UAT remains optional/non-blocking. 3 atomic commits (4b369efe RED card test, f725935a 3 components GREEN, 84c74ac3 mount + chip + layout); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks. CREDITUI-01/02 marked complete. **Phase 115 now 2/2 plans — COMPLETE.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 115`, then Phase 116 (Calibration & Charge-On Validation — the LATE phase that derives grant/markup/price from measured cost + gates real charging ON). See 115-02-SUMMARY.md.

Status (115-01, Wave 1 — CREDITUI-01/02): shipped — the owner-facing credit-balance DATA layer (no UI; Plan 02 consumes it). **CREDITUI-01 (Task 2, GREEN):** NEW `lib/queries/credits.ts` (`import 'server-only'`) `getCreditOverview(companyId) → { balance, history[], lowBalanceThresholds }` — `requireServiceClient()` + `Promise.all` of: `companies.credit_balance` (`.single()`), the credit_ledger owner-safe projection, and `getBillingConfig()`. **CARDINAL RULE — owner-safe projection by construction:** the ledger SELECT is a fixed `OWNER_SAFE_LEDGER_COLUMNS = 'operation_type, delta_credits, reason, created_at'` const (`.select(OWNER_SAFE_LEDGER_COLUMNS).eq('company_id').order('created_at',{ascending:false}).limit(50)`) — `real_cost_usd`/`markup`/`balance_after`/`idempotency_key`/`ref_id`/`id`/`company_id` are NEVER selected, so the rows are safe to hand a client component (defense-in-depth, never token math). Null-safe: missing company row → balance 0, never throws. Company-scoped (companyId, not userId) so Plan-02's settings page + an optional header chip both call it. **CREDITUI-02 (Task 3, GREEN):** NEW export `notifyLowCreditBalance({companyId,userId,previousBalance,newBalance,thresholds})` in `lib/billing/credit-ledger.ts`, mirroring `notifyQuotaThresholds` — fires ONLY on a DOWNWARD crossing, deduped per company+threshold+month. Zero/exhausted (`prev>0 && new<=0`) → `quota.exhausted` (both channels), dedupe `credit-zero-{company}-{month}`; a low non-zero crossing → `quota.80pct` once for the HIGHEST threshold crossed (`[...thresholds].sort desc`, `.find(t>0 && prev>t && new<=t)`), dedupe `credit-low-{company}-{t}-{month}`; `linkUrl:'/settings/billing'`. Whole body try/catch → NEVER throws. Local private `monthKey()` (`YYYY-MM` UTC) copied into the file (NOT imported from quota.ts — avoid coupling). Reuses EXISTING events `quota.80pct`/`quota.exhausted` — **NO new EventType** (`event-types.ts` byte-unchanged). Copy INFORMATIONAL only (enforcement OFF this milestone) — grep `blocked|denied|cannot` in credit-ledger.ts → 0 (reworded my own doc-comment to avoid the literal). **Wired into recordCreditDebit:** a `void notifyLowCreditBalance({companyId, userId:null, previousBalance:current, newBalance:balanceAfter, thresholds:cfg.lowBalanceThresholds})` immediately after the existing `companies.credit_balance` update, still inside the existing try (`cfg`/`current`/`balanceAfter` already in scope). `void`-ed so a notify delay never blocks the debit return; debit math/idempotency/ledger insert/balance write byte-unchanged — purely additive at the tail. **Wave-0 TDD:** NEW `tests/unit/queries/credits-query.test.ts` (5 — balance read, history mapping, the owner-safe SELECT-STRING guard `expect(ledgerSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith(stringContaining('operation_type'))` + `.not.toContain('real_cost_usd')`/`.not.toContain('markup')` proving the rule from the SELECT not from hiding, thresholds, missing-row→0) + NEW `tests/unit/billing/credit-low-notify.test.ts` (4 — low downward-crossing fires once with the right dedupe key, zero fires `quota.exhausted`, no-cross silent, notify-throws swallowed). **DEVIATION (1, Rule 3 — pre-declared allowlist pattern):** `lib/queries/credits.ts` is a NEW `getBillingConfig` consumer (reads `lowBalanceThresholds`), turning the Phase-111 `BILLCFG-03` dormancy guard (`tests/unit/billing/billing-config.test.ts`) red; added `CREDITS_QUERY_PATH` to the `ALLOWLIST` (same move every prior v4.7 plan applied — the guard still fails on any OTHER reference). `npx vitest run tests/unit/queries tests/unit/billing` → 27 files / 182 passed. FULL `npx vitest run` → **295 files passed | 3 skipped, 2090 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** — no regressions; the known parallel-only `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` flake did not surface this run. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` → no errors in the touched/created files. No migration, no env var, no secret. 4 atomic commits (d42c45cd RED scaffolds, e4ce764b getCreditOverview, 6e379312 notify hook, cecb2d10 BILLCFG-03 allowlist); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks (placeholder ids only). CREDITUI-01/02 marked complete. **Phase 115 now 1/2 plans.** Next: `/gsd:execute-phase 115` (Plan 115-02 — the owner-facing balance widget + consumption history UI that consumes getCreditOverview). See 115-01-SUMMARY.md.

Status (114-03, Wave 2 — DISCLOSE-01): shipped — **Phase 114 COMPLETE (3/3 plans) — the LAST plan; the estimate-payment-fee track (fee + gating + disclosure) is fully executed.** Fee disclosure at the Stripe connection moment, locked to the live `billing_config` so one number drives both the charge (FEE-03) and the disclosure. **Task 1 (card disclosure):** `components/settings/stripe-connect-card.tsx` `StripeConnectCard` gains a REQUIRED `feePct: number` prop; body derives `const feeWhole = feePct * 100; const feeLabel = \`${Number.isInteger(feeWhole) ? feeWhole : feeWhole.toFixed(2)}%\`` (0.01→"1%", 0.02→"2%", 0.005→"0.5%"). The `not_connected` `<CardContent>` branch renders a `data-testid="fee-disclosure"` amber-accented `<p>` ("Xtimator charges a {feeLabel} fee on each payment you receive through the platform | this is separate from Stripe's processing fees.") via the existing `t()` interpolation — directly below the connect copy. Scoped to `not_connected` ONLY (not_configured/connected untouched); NO hard-coded "1%" in the copy (grep `[^0-9]1%[^0-9]|charges a 1%` → 0; even reworded the doc-comment to avoid a literal). **Task 2 (server read):** `app/(app)/settings/payments/page.tsx` imports `getBillingConfig`, reads `const { estimateFeePct } = await getBillingConfig()` before the return, threads `<StripeConnectCard state={state} feePct={estimateFeePct} />`. NO hard-coded fee, NO new company column, the three-state `state` derivation (114-02) untouched. The dormancy guard stayed green via the 114-01-PRE-ADDED allowlist entry at `billing-config.test.ts:220` — verified present, NOT re-touched. **Task 3 (TDD, config-driven proof):** NEW `tests/unit/settings/payments-disclosure.test.tsx` (zero overlap with 114-01/114-02 files) mirrors `payments-page.test.tsx`'s mock+render convention + mocks `getBillingConfig`: 0.02 + NOT_CONNECTED → HTML has "2%" + "separate from Stripe" + `data-testid="fee-disclosure"`; 0.01 → "1%" (same path, different config → different render); 0.02 + CONNECTED → disclosure ABSENT. **DEVIATION (1, Rule 1 — break-fix my own change caused):** Task 2 made `payments/page.tsx` a NEW `getBillingConfig` consumer, which crashed the 114-02-owned `payments-page.test.tsx` render tests ("No createServiceClient export on the mock" — the real server-only reader calls `createServiceClient`, absent from that file's `@/lib/supabase/service` mock); added a `getBillingConfig` mock STUB (`mockResolvedValue({ estimateFeePct: 0.01 })`) to that file — NO disclosure assertions added (those stay solely in the new file, honoring the no-overlap rule); the 3 render tests pass unchanged (commit 3ebf329f). `npx vitest run` of the 3 verification suites (disclosure + billing-config + payments-page) → 3 files / 26 passed. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` → no new errors in the 2 touched source files. FULL `npx vitest run` → **292 files passed | 1 failed (2080 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo)** = the known PARALLEL-ONLY `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` GET-405 flake (re-confirmed 8/8 in isolation, no Phase-114 file; out-of-scope, in deferred-items.md). No migration, no env var, no secret; fee computation (114-01) + gating predicate (114-02) untouched (read-only `estimateFeePct` dependency). 4 atomic commits (6911aec9 card disclosure, be338989 server read, 2a383efe disclosure test, 3ebf329f mock fix); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks (placeholder `ca_test_123`/`acct_abc` ids in tests only). DISCLOSE-01 marked complete. **Phase 114 now 3/3 plans — FEE-01..04 + PAYGATE-01/02 + DISCLOSE-01 all complete.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 114`. See 114-03-SUMMARY.md.

Status (114-02, Wave 1 — PAYGATE-01/02): shipped — total payment-UI gating over the forward-looking surfaces. **PAYGATE-01 (Task 1, TDD):** NEW `lib/billing/payments-enabled.ts` — PURE module (NO `import 'server-only'`, no I/O) `paymentsEnabled(company)` → `Boolean(company.stripe_account_id) && company.stripe_connect_status === 'active'`. Pure so the server computes it and threads the boolean to a client surface as a prop, AND so it is unit-testable. NEW `tests/unit/billing/payments-enabled.test.ts` (5 cases — active/disconnected/pending/null-account/null-status). `lib/actions/invoice.ts` `generateInvoice` step-4 Connect check refactored from the inline `!company?.stripe_account_id || company.stripe_connect_status !== 'active'` to `!company || !paymentsEnabled(company)` (same message, same position, same selected columns) — NO `=== 'active'` payment literal remains (only a doc-comment). **PAYGATE-02 (Task 2):** `app/(app)/projects/[id]/page.tsx` company query widened with `stripe_account_id, stripe_connect_status`; `const canIssueInvoice = paymentsEnabled(...)` computed server-side and threaded `ProjectWorkspace → OverviewTab → EstimateTab → EstimateEditor` (the REAL chain — one level deeper than the plan's page→OverviewTab→editor sketch; all 4 intermediates updated, tsc confirms no missed render site). Editor dialog gate changed `{isCurrent && (` → `{isCurrent && paymentsEnabled && (` around `<GenerateInvoiceDialog/>` (disconnected = NO orphan). `IssuedInvoicesPanel` stays UNGATED — historical RECORD, returns null when empty (locked decision: gate AFFORDANCES, not history; Paid badge keyed on payment_status also exempt). **Two-state coverage (Task 3, TDD):** `tests/unit/settings/payments-page.test.tsx` gains a PAYGATE-02 block (predicate OFF disconnected/never-connected, ON active); NEW `tests/unit/settings/editor-payment-gating.test.tsx` (4 cases — the exact `{isCurrent && paymentsEnabled}` guard renders only when active+current, not disconnected/never-connected/non-current; asserts the gating boolean rather than full-rendering the heavy client editor — the deterministic seam); `tests/e2e/estimate-share-payment.spec.ts` Scenario A already proves disconnected share = no `Pay $`/`Powered by Stripe` (surface 3, naturally gated) — added a comment documenting the record-vs-affordance decision. **Scope note (NOT a deviation):** `app/(app)/settings/payments/page.tsx` keeps its own three-way `not_configured/connected/not_connected` state machine (inventory surface 4 — the gate's OWN control panel, Plan-03's scope), NOT a forward affordance, so it is intentionally untouched. `npx vitest run` of the 4 plan suites → 20/20. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean on the 5 touched source files. FULL `npx vitest run` → **291 files passed | 1 failed (2077 passed)** = the pre-existing PARALLEL-ONLY `mcp-route-contract.test.ts` GET-405 flake (re-confirmed 8/8 in isolation, touches no Phase-114 file; out-of-scope). 3 atomic commits (7f16528c predicate+refactor, 91e696f1 affordance gating, 047e07d4 two-state tests); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks (placeholder Stripe ids only). 0 behavior deviations (only the deeper-than-sketched prop chain noted). PAYGATE-01/02 marked complete. **Phase 114 now 2/3 plans.** Next: `/gsd:execute-phase 114` (Plan 114-03 — fee disclosure at Connect, DISCLOSE-01). See 114-02-SUMMARY.md.

Status (114-01, Wave 1 — FEE-01/02/03/04): shipped — the **1% estimate application fee is live** on the single real customer-payment surface (the Stripe Connect hosted invoice). **FEE-04 (Task 1, TDD):** NEW `lib/billing/estimate-fee.ts` — PURE module (NO `import 'server-only'`, no DB read, reusable by any future pay-route) `computeApplicationFee(amountCents, feePct, minCents)`: `amount<=0||pct<=0 → 0`, else `Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(amount*pct), minCents), amount-1)`. Integer cents, floored at min, **clamped STRICTLY below the charge** (Stripe rejects fee>=charge); the 1-cent edge yields `amount-1=0` so the caller omits the field. NEW `tests/unit/billing/estimate-fee.test.ts` (10 cases — the full math + 1-cent/zero/neg/zero-pct ladder). **FEE-01 (Task 2, TDD):** `lib/billing/invoice-service.ts` `createConnectInvoice` opts gain a REQUIRED `applicationFeeCents` (0 = omit); `stripe.invoices.create` conditionally spreads `application_fee_amount` ONLY when `>0` (Pitfall 1 — Stripe rejects a \$0 fee). Fee rides on the **Invoice** object, NEVER the InvoiceItem (Pitfall 2); `idempotencyBase` byte-unchanged (Pitfall 6); the `{ stripeAccount }` reqOpt already supplies the Direct-Charge header. The old "OMITS everywhere" test split into omitted-when-0 + present-on-invoice-when>0 (250). **FEE-03 (Task 3):** `lib/actions/invoice.ts` `generateInvoice`, after the `amountCents<=0` guard (amount now >0), reads `{ estimateFeePct, estimateFeeMinCents }` from `getBillingConfig()` (runtime, never hard-coded, applies w/o deploy) → `computeApplicationFee(...)` → threads `applicationFeeCents` into `createConnectInvoice`. **FEE-02 documented satisfied-by-FEE-01** in an in-code note: the Phase-70 standalone estimate checkout pay-route no longer exists (superseded by Phase-94 hosted invoices); the invoice path is the single customer-payment surface; subscription/top-up checkouts are platform-account charges and carry NO fee (verified — `grep application_fee_amount app/api/billing/create-checkout-session create-topup-session` → NOTHING). **DEVIATION (0 — pre-declared pattern, not a deviation):** extended the Phase-111 `BILLCFG-03` dormancy allowlist (`tests/unit/billing/billing-config.test.ts`) for BOTH new `getBillingConfig` consumers — `lib/actions/invoice.ts` (this plan) + `app/(app)/settings/payments/page.tsx` (Plan-03 disclosure %, added pre-emptively so the guard stays green when Plan 03 lands; the Set tolerates the not-yet-referencing path); the guard still fails on any OTHER consumer. `npx vitest run tests/unit/billing tests/unit/actions/invoice.test.ts` → **21 files / 160 passed**; the 4 plan-touched files → 40/40. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean on the 3 touched source files. FULL `npx vitest run` → 289 passed | **1 failed** = `tests/unit/mcp-route-contract.test.ts` GET-405 — a pre-existing PARALLEL-ONLY flake (passes 8/8 in isolation), touches no Phase-114 file; logged out-of-scope to `deferred-items.md`, not fixed (scope boundary). 3 atomic commits (b5c749bb FEE-04 helper, 1fd7f787 FEE-01 invoice field, 294a2754 FEE-03 action+allowlist); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks (placeholder Stripe ids only). FEE-01/02/03/04 marked complete. **Phase 114 now 1/3 plans.** Next: `/gsd:execute-phase 114` (Plan 114-02 — total payment-UI gating, PAYGATE-01/02). See 114-01-SUMMARY.md.

Status (113-03, Wave 1 — TOPUP-02 route + TOPUP-03 + MIG-01): shipped — **Phase 113 COMPLETE (3/3 plans) — the LAST plan; the Stripe rail is fully wired.** Two additive features flip the last 113-01 RED files GREEN, no overlap with 113-02. **TOPUP-02 (route side):** NEW `app/api/billing/create-topup-session/route.ts` — a near-copy of `create-checkout-session` keeping the auth 401 / `demoGuardResponse()` / company lookup (`select('id, stripe_customer_id').eq('user_id', claims.sub).single()`) / `getStripeClient()` blocks IDENTICAL, but `mode:'payment'` + inline `line_items[0].price_data` (`currency:'usd'`, `unit_amount:pack.priceCents`, `product_data.name`) + `metadata:{ type:'credit_topup', companyId:company.id, credits:String(pack.credits) }`. The pack is looked up SERVER-SIDE from `getBillingConfig().topUpPacks[packIndex]` (`Number.isInteger` guard) — credits/price NEVER trusted from the body (Pitfall 4); bad packIndex → 400 (no `sessions.create`), unauth → 401. NO pre-created Stripe Price (inline price_data only). 3/3 topup-checkout tests green. **TOPUP-03 (affordance PATH):** NEW pure `lib/billing/overage-affordance.ts` `buildOverageAffordance(check)` — `shortfall>0` → `{ topUpUrl:'/settings/billing?topup=1', upgradeUrl:'/settings/billing' }`, `shortfall===0` → `null`; keys on `shortfall` independent of `allowed`, so an enforcement-off shortfall still yields an affordance (proving TOPUP-03 is a path, not a block). 3/3 overage tests green. **MIG-01:** the existing `generate-estimate` count-based 402 (`if (!allowed)` after `checkQuota(supabase, companyId, 'estimate')`) is ENRICHED in place — `checkCredits(supabase, companyId)` + `buildOverageAffordance` spread-merge a `topUpUrl` field onto the SAME response (`...(affordance ? { topUpUrl } : {})`); the checkQuota call + its condition are byte-unchanged, NO `credit.allowed` gating path added; `checkCredits` is enforcement-off (always `allowed:true`) so it can never block. `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean on all 3 touched source files. **DEVIATION (1, Rule 3 — pre-declared pattern):** extended the Phase-111 `BILLCFG-03` dormancy guard (`tests/unit/billing/billing-config.test.ts`) allowlist to cover the two Stripe-rail config consumers — the 113-02 webhook grant arm (`app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts`, reads `cfg.tiers`) and the new `create-topup-session` route (reads `topUpPacks`); both are runtime-authoritative billing readers, the guard still fails on any OTHER consumer (identical to 112-03's credit-ledger allowlisting). FULL `npx vitest run` → **289 files passed | 3 skipped, 2054 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (113-02 baseline 288/2053; +1 file / +1 — the previously-RED topup/overage sets now green, plus the guard fix). `quota.test.ts` + `quota-price-research.test.ts` → 17/17 (count gate untouched). 3 atomic commits (a09fe12 top-up route, 6f62056 affordance+402 enrich, 72f35836 BILLCFG-03 allowlist); all normal hooked (gitleaks ran, no `--no-verify`), no leaks found (placeholder Stripe ids only). TOPUP-02/03 + MIG-01 marked complete. Enforcement stays OFF until Phase 116 calibration by design. **Phase 113 now 3/3 plans — the Stripe rail track (grants + top-ups + parallel-run affordance) is fully executed.** Next: `/gsd:verify-work 113`, then `/gsd:execute-phase 114` (estimate payment fee + payment-UI gating + disclosure — independent of the credit track). See 113-03-SUMMARY.md.

Status (113-02, Wave 1 — TOPUP-01/02 + MIG-01): shipped — the Stripe RAIL is live. Two additive edits to ONE file (`app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts`), flipping the 113-01 RED webhook suite GREEN (5 failed/2 passed → 7/7). **TOPUP-01 (invoice.paid grant):** after the existing `tier_renews_at` update, resolve `companies.id + tier` by `stripe_subscription_id` (`select('id, tier').eq('stripe_subscription_id', subId).maybeSingle()`), read `cfg.tiers[tier]?.monthlyCreditGrant ?? 0` from `getBillingConfig()` (runtime-authoritative — NEVER hard-coded, NEVER entitlements.ts; tier from `companies.tier` because `metadata.plan` is absent on invoice.paid, Pitfall 6), and `grantCredits({ companyId, credits: grant, reason:'grant', refId: invoice.id, idempotencyKey: event.id })`. ONE hook covers first-subscribe AND renewal. Free tier → 0 (`grantCredits` no-ops on `<=0`). Double-layer idempotency: a redelivered event short-circuits at the `processed_stripe_events` 23505 dedup gate (returns 200, never reaches the grant); the `grantCredits` idempotencyKey is the second layer. Grant ONLY here — never in checkout (Pitfall 2 double-grant). **TOPUP-02 (webhook arm):** new arm at the TOP of `checkout.session.completed`, BEFORE the `!companyId || session.mode !== 'subscription'` early-break (Pitfall 3 — the arm would never run after it), firing on `session.metadata?.type === 'credit_topup' && session.payment_status === 'paid'`: parse the STRING `metadata.credits` via `Number(...)` (Pitfall 5), `if (topupCompanyId && credits > 0)` → `grantCredits({ reason:'topup', refId: session.id, idempotencyKey: event.id })`, then `break` so a top-up session never falls into the subscription update path. **MIG-01:** additive only — the count-based subscription update, `subId` resolution, and `subscriptions.retrieve` cast are byte-unchanged; no `maxEstimatesPerMonth` gating; static guard passes (`grantCredits` present). `npx vitest run tests/unit/webhooks/stripe-credit-grant.test.ts` → 7/7. Candidate set = post-anchor `price_source === 'ai_estimate'` items ONLY — `price_book` (authoritative) + owner-edited (`price_source` null) items are NEVER touched (RPRICE-03 runtime precedence). Per candidate: `cache.get` HIT (positive price) → stage a `researched` re-tag with NO provider call + NO allowance (a hit is free; `getPriceResearchProvider` is only resolved when there is a miss AND quota allows). Misses → `checkQuota('price_research')`: `allowed:false` SKIPS the provider for the WHOLE miss set (items keep `ai_estimate`, the call still returns sections — RMETER-03 over-allowance skip, never a hard fail); `allowed:true` → `getPriceResearchProvider()` (null → safe no-op) → ONE batched `lookup(misses.map(name), region, currency)` (Pitfall 4). Per result: `recordUsage(supabase, companyId, 'price_researched', 1, idemKey)` (idempotent, best-effort try/catch) where `idemKey = ${attemptId ?? projectId ?? companyId}:research:${normalizeServiceNameKey(name)}:${normalizeRegion(region)}` — the **Warning-#1 fix**: a per-generation-attempt seed (retry-stable for the same attempt, distinct across estimates so research is not under-metered across estimates); `attemptId` + `projectId` context params EXIST so 108-04 can pass the best available token; the project-scoped fallback is finer than company-scoped and the cache-overlap is benign (a repeat same-service+region within the 30-day TTL is a cache HIT → no allowance regardless of seed). Then the EVIDENCE GATE (RPRICE-04): re-tag `ai_estimate → researched` with `result.unit_price` AND `cache.put` ONLY when `isUsableCandidate(result)` (real source_url + snippet + positive price); an unevidenced result KEEPS `ai_estimate` (no put, no re-tag). Staged re-tags applied to an immutable section tree (identity-keyed `Map<LineItemOutput,LineItemOutput>`, mirroring `anchorAndClampSections`). NEVER-$0 LADDER (RFALL-01): after re-tag, items still at `unit_price<=0` (an `ai_estimate` item the model priced $0 with no research improvement) are COUNTED in `flaggedUnpriced` — KEPT (not mutated to a fake price, not dropped). The whole flow is wrapped in try/catch returning the ORIGINAL sections + a best-effort flagged count (never breaks generation). **Flagged-unpriced mechanism (Claude's discretion):** NO new `price_source` enum value (DB CHECK is price_book|ai_estimate|researched only — Phase 105); the $0-item keeps `ai_estimate` + its model price and the ONLY signal is the `flaggedUnpriced` integer in `ResearchOutcome { sections, flaggedUnpriced }` — 108-04 sets `awaiting_details` when `flaggedUnpriced>0 AND total>0` (exactly what the 108-02 gate permits) so priced lines persist while the unpriced one routes to the existing needs-details recourse. NEW `tests/unit/estimate/price-research-orchestrator.test.ts` (10 tests, mocked provider/cache/quota, no network/secrets, `https://example.test/...` URLs): contract shape, never-throws on rejecting deps, no-candidate short-circuit (byte-identical, provider never touched), precedence (only the ai_estimate item searched), cache HIT (no provider/recordUsage), MISS+allowed+evidenced (re-tag+put+recordUsage key contains attemptId+normName+region), evidence gate (unevidenced keeps ai_estimate, no put), over-allowance skip, never-$0 flagged count (kept not dropped), batched (ONE lookup for N) + idempotency-key stable across re-runs. `generate-estimate.ts` UNTOUCHED (108-04 wires it). `tsc --noEmit` clean on both files (the 12 full-repo tsc errors — 5 stale `Entitlements` mocks from 108-01, 7 long-standing tsconfig/es2018 mismatches — are pre-existing in unrelated files, logged to `deferred-items.md`, NOT in my files; CI uses scoped `tsconfig.ci.json`). FULL `npx vitest run` → **273 files passed | 3 skipped, 1915 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (no regressions vs the 271/1899 108-01 baseline; +1 file / +16 assertions). 2 atomic commits (7a72b8f contract+Tests-1-3, d2e1203 body+Tests-4-10); gitleaks ran on both (normal hooked commits — no `--no-verify`), no leaks found. 1 deviation (Rule 1 — reworded the channel-neutrality doc comment so the `lib/whatsapp` grep returns 0 while the module genuinely imports no channel package). RPRICE-01 / RPRICE-03 / RPRICE-04 / RFALL-01 marked complete. **Phase 108 now 3/5 plans.** Next: `/gsd:execute-phase 108` (Plan 108-04 — wire `researchUnmatchedPrices` into `generateEstimateForProject` after `anchorAndClampSections`/before totals; researched prices flow into totals; flaggedUnpriced>0 + total>0 → awaiting_details). See 108-03-SUMMARY.md.
Status (108-02, Wave 1 — RFALL-02): shipped — the vagueness-gate refinement (the Ellen rule, refined). `isVagueEstimate` (`lib/estimate/quality/vagueness.ts`) now DOCUMENTS the empty-vs-flagged distinction and the contract is LOCKED by 6 tests; the function BODY is functionally identical (`!hasTotal || !hasItems`). The key insight: the gate keys on the AGGREGATE `total`, not per-item prices, so a partially-priced estimate (total>0) carrying a flagged unpriced line (just another item, possibly $0) already passes today and a genuinely valueless (empty / all-$0 → total 0) estimate still blocks → needs-details. The plan therefore deliberately added NO per-item $0 branch (that would re-block partially-priced estimates / the never-$0 fallback ladder Plans 108-03/04) — the doc comment warns a future reader against "fixing" it back. Model via `getOpenRouterDefaultModel()` + sane fallback. Reuses `getIntegrationKey('openrouter')` (no new credential/dep). Builds the query through `buildResearchSearchPrompt` (hardened boundary), parses the model JSON from `message.content`, and enforces the citation cross-check EVIDENCE GATE: a self-asserted `source_url` is trusted ONLY when it matches a real `annotations[].url_citation.url`, snippet pulled from that annotation's `content`; uncited entries are nulled so `isUsableCandidate` rejects them. `priceResearchPayloadSchema.safeParse` before trust; best-effort langfuse trace; wrapped try/catch → all-misses on ANY error (missing key → no fetch). **Anthropic-web (GATED quality-fallback, non-default)** `lib/estimate/price-research/adapters/anthropic-web.ts` `makeAnthropicWebProvider()` constructs `new Anthropic({apiKey})` (SDK already present) and issues ONE `messages.create` with `tools:[{ type:'web_search_20250305', name:'web_search', max_uses:5, user_location:{ type:'approximate', city:region.city??undefined, region:region.state??undefined, country:'US' } }]`, model `claude-sonnet-4-20250514`. Detects a `web_search_tool_result_error` block (→ all misses), indexes the text blocks' `citations[].url → cited_text`, cross-checks each `source_url` (uncited → null), zod-validates, never throws (missing key → SDK never constructed). Doc comment marks Anthropic as the FALLBACK vendor — hierarchy not inverted. Both adapters channel-neutral (`grep -rc "lib/whatsapp" lib/estimate/price-research/adapters/` → 0/0); `generate-estimate.ts` UNTOUCHED; `package.json` NOT modified (no new dep). 2 NEW test files (19 assertions): openrouter-web (11 — separate-call shape/no tool_choice, engine exa-default/native/unrecognized→exa, evidence gate cited→usable/uncited→null/one-miss-per-item, degraded missing-key/thrown/non-ok/malformed/empty) + anthropic-web (8 — web_search_20250305 + user_location city/region/country, citations→usable, error→miss, missing-key→no-SDK, thrown, empty, null-region→undefined). Mocked fetch / mocked SDK class — ZERO live network, placeholder keys (`sk-test-or-placeholder`, `sk-ant-test-placeholder`), gitleaks clean. FULL `npx vitest run` → **268 files passed | 3 skipped, 1872 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo** (no regressions vs the 266/1853 107-01 baseline; +2 files / +19 assertions). `tsc --noEmit` clean on all 4 files. 2 atomic commits (50a397f OpenRouter-web adapter+tests, af14910 Anthropic-web adapter+tests); gitleaks ran on both (normal hooked commits — no `--no-verify`), no leaks found. 2 deviations auto-fixed (Rule 3 — reworded adapter doc comments so the forbidden-token `tool_choice|create_estimate|:online|plugins` acceptance greps return 0 while the request body stays correct; Rule 1 — test-infra corrections: `vi.hoisted` for mock vars, `{ metadata }`-wrapped Supabase row, constructable class-based Anthropic SDK mock). NEW `lib/estimate/price-research/cache.ts` — tenant-scoped `get(companyId, name, region, currency)` / `put(input)` over `public.price_research_cache` via `requireServiceClient` (3 refs). `PRICE_RESEARCH_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000` module const. `get()` builds the lookup key via the Plan 106-01 `normalizeServiceNameKey` + `normalizeRegion` (`from './normalize'`), `.maybeSingle()`s the 4-tuple, returns the NEUTRAL `CachedPriceDatum` when present AND `expires_at >= now`, else `null` (expired = MISS; missing = MISS). **No provider seam at all** — a HIT is a pure service-role DB read, so the cache can never invoke research on a hit (leakage + no-search guarantee is structural). `put()` stamps `expires_at = now + PRICE_RESEARCH_TTL_MS` and upserts on `onConflict: 'company_id,normalized_name,region,currency_code'`. The returned `CachedPriceDatum` interface lists ONLY `{ unit_price, currency, source, confidence?, expires_at }` — `company_id` appears ONLY in `.eq`/`.upsert` KEY usage + comments, never in the value shape (leakage-guard contract; a typed literal in the test makes a future leaky-field addition a TS error too). Extended vitest `include` with `'tests/eval/**/*.test.ts'` (scoped to *.test.ts so helper modules are never collected as suites) — the load-bearing gate-teeth fix: a `vitest run tests/eval` path arg FILTERS against `include` (does NOT override it), so without this entry every eval command silently found "No test files found"/exit-0/zero-tests and EVAL-02/03/04 would never catch a regression. Proven collected: `vitest run tests/eval` reports Test Files 2 / 20 tests. EVAL-01: 6 typed golden fixtures (audio-deck-rebuild, photo-roof-repair, text-fence-paint, mixed-kitchen-reno, vague-do-some-work, schema-drift-guard) in tests/eval/fixtures/{types,cases}.ts — synthetic, no network, gitleaks-clean. Isolation discipline (Pitfall 5): per-file vi.setConfig({testTimeout:30_000}) + afterEach clearAllMocks/resetModules + lazy `await import` inside the per-case runner — resetModules is safe here (each case's await import + invoke fully resolves within the test body, so it never evicts a mid-flight dynamic import, the 103-01 failure mode). DETERMINISM GATE HELD: full `npx vitest run` 3x identical GREEN (250 files / 1732 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo — was 248/1712 in 103-01 → +2 eval files / +20 eval tests, no contamination). 2 atomic commits: efc32d1 (fixtures+metrics+include), 3b9a0bc (mock-providers+harness). Prior status: 103-01 shipped — the full `npx vitest run` suite is now deterministic-GREEN 3x consecutively (1712 passed | 2 skipped | 33 todo; 248 files), the Wave-1 exit criterion. Root-caused the long-standing full-suite flakiness as IMPORT LATENCY under vitest forks-pool worker contention — NOT a mock-state leak (the working hypothesis in 103-RESEARCH). vitest 4's default `pool: 'forks'` co-schedules many files; the first runtime load of the heavy LangGraph/AI-provider/Inngest module trees (loaded via dynamic `import`, several via the Wave-0 `import(/* @vite-ignore */ spec)` scaffold) can exceed the 5s default `testTimeout`; a timed-out `graph.invoke()` then leaves a pending async that fires a module-scope spy AFTER the next test → the spurious "called 2 times" count cascade in the whatsapp files. PROOF it is latency not leakage: at `testTimeout=10000` the whole estimate+inngest+services+whatsapp cohort is green 3x with zero count corruption. Fix = per-file `vi.setConfig({ testTimeout: 30_000, hookTimeout: 30_000 })` on exactly the heavy-runtime-import test files (Class B) — test-authoring level, NOT a vitest.config global flag, NOT clearMocks/mockReset/restoreMocks, NOT sharding/--no-file-parallelism, no skip/delete (`git diff vitest.config.ts` empty). The planned `afterEach(resetModules)` discipline was tried and REJECTED on evidence: it deadlocked the `@vite-ignore` dynamic-import victims and spread timeouts (the diagnosis's own "blanket resetModules made it worse" warning). Also repaired 7 deterministic stale pre-existing tests (Class A) — 3 generate-estimate route + capture-lineage (mock `@/lib/queries/active-company` cookies path), stripe-webhook (`.select()` chain for the new subscription.deleted pre-lookup), onboarding-survey (localStorage teardown), theme-toggle (dropdown is light↔dark only → Moon for 'system', not Monitor), jobs-status (force non-dev INNGEST_DEV), landing-actions (objectContaining for the new step/featureImageUrls success fields), phase83 guard (refined to allow staff.ts's legitimate company_members owner-role check — surfaced + verified NOT a product bug). 3 atomic commits: eb0a4cd + b8b0020 (Class A), 6b7fffc (Class B). Production source untouched (only the two pre-existing non-test working-tree files remain, not mine); xphere untouched. See 103-01-SUMMARY.md. Wave 2 (eval harness) + Wave 3 (CI gate) now unblocked.
